    function showToast(msg) {
      const el = document.getElementById('toast');
      el.textContent = msg;
      el.style.display = 'block';
      clearTimeout(window.__toastTimer);
      window.__toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        el.style.display = 'none';
      }, 1700);
    }

    function copyCode(code) {
      const clean = String(code || '').replace(/[†*]/g, '').trim();
      if (!clean) return;
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(clean).catch(() => {});
      }
      showToast(`คัดลอกรหัส ${clean} แล้ว`);
    }

    function switchMode(mode) {
      currentMode = mode;
      document.getElementById('btnVol3').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'VOL3');
      document.getElementById('btnTabular').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'TABULAR');

      if (mode === 'VOL3' && !vol3Loaded) {
        loadVol3Data();
      } else {
        filterData();
      }
    }

    async function loadVol3Data() {
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('statusText');
      statusEl.textContent = 'กำลังโหลดคลัง Vol. 3...';

      const candidateUrls = [
        './icd10_vol3_index.json',
        './data/icd10_vol3_index.json',
        './vol3_index.json'
      ];

      for (const url of candidateUrls) {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store' });
          if (res.ok) {
            const json = await res.json();
            vol3Data = Array.isArray(json) ? json : (json.items || json.data || []);
            vol3Loaded = true;
            statusEl.textContent = `Vol. 3 พร้อมใช้งาน (${vol3Data.length.toLocaleString()} รายการ)`;
            filterData();
            return;
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // ลอง path ถัดไป
        }
      }

      vol3Data = sampleTabular.map((item) => ({
        code: item.code,
        term: item.title,
        subterm: item.sub || '',
        chapter: item.category
      }));
      vol3Loaded = true;
      statusEl.textContent = `Vol. 3 โหมดสำรอง (${vol3Data.length} รายการ)`;
      filterData();
    }

    function resetFilters() {
      document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('chapterFilter').value = 'ALL';
      filterData();
    }

  function escapeHtml(str) {
    return String(str || '')
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
  }

    function filterData() {
      const q = (document.getElementById('searchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const ch = document.getElementById('chapterFilter').value;
      const resultsEl = document.getElementById('results');
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('statusText');

      if (currentMode === 'TABULAR') {
        const filtered = sampleTabular.filter((item) => {
          const matchCh = (ch === 'ALL') || (item.category === ch);
          if (!matchCh) return false;
          if (!q) return true;
          const hay = `${item.code} ${item.title} ${item.sub || ''} ${item.note || ''}`.toLowerCase();
          return hay.includes(q);
        });

        statusEl.textContent = `Tabular: แสดง ${filtered.length} รายการ`;

        if (!filtered.length) {
          resultsEl.innerHTML = `<div class="note-box">ไม่พบรายการที่ตรงกับคำค้นหา "${escapeHtml(q)}"</div>`;
          return;
        }

        resultsEl.innerHTML = filtered.map((item) => {
          const btnClass = item.isHeader ? 'code-btn' : 'code-btn sub';
          return `
            <div class="row-item ${item.isHeader ? 'is-header' : ''}">
              <div>
                <button type="button" class="${btnClass}" onclick="copyCode('${escapeHtml(item.code)}')" title="คลิกเพื่อคัดลอกรหัส">
                  ${escapeHtml(item.code)}
                </button>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div class="term-title">${escapeHtml(item.title)}</div>
                ${item.sub ? `<div class="term-sub">${escapeHtml(item.sub)}</div>` : ''}
                ${item.note ? `<div class="note-box">${escapeHtml(item.note)}</div>` : ''}
                ${item.tag ? `<div class="flag-tag">${escapeHtml(item.tag)}</div>` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      } else {
        const filtered = vol3Data.filter((item) => {
          const itemCh = item.chapter || item.category || 'Ch1';
          const matchCh = (ch === 'ALL') || (itemCh === ch);
          if (!matchCh) return false;
          if (!q) return true;
          const hay = `${item.code || ''} ${item.term || item.title || ''} ${item.subterm || item.sub || ''}`.toLowerCase();
          return hay.includes(q);
        });

        const capped = filtered.slice(0, 250);
        statusEl.textContent = `Vol. 3: พบ ${filtered.length.toLocaleString()} รายการ (แสดง ${capped.length})`;

        if (!capped.length) {
          resultsEl.innerHTML = `<div class="note-box">ไม่พบดัชนี Vol. 3 ที่ตรงกับ "${escapeHtml(q)}"</div>`;
          return;
        }

        resultsEl.innerHTML = capped.map((item) => {
          const code = item.code || '';
          const title = item.term || item.title || '';
          const sub = item.subterm || item.sub || '';
          return `
            <div class="row-item">
              <div>
                <button type="button" class="code-btn sub" onclick="copyCode('${escapeHtml(code)}')" title="คลิกเพื่อคัดลอกรหัส">
                  ${escapeHtml(code || '—')}
                </button>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div class="term-title">${escapeHtml(title)}</div>
                ${sub ? `<div class="term-sub">${escapeHtml(sub)}</div>` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('');
      }
    }

    filterData();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
